Guardian "1" o-- "*" Application : ParentId
Student "1" o-- "*" Application : convertedToStudentId
Student "1" o-- "*" Guardian : studentId
Guardian "1" o-- "*" Guardian : guardianId
Student "1" o-- "*" Enrollment : studentId
ClassSection "1" o-- "*" Enrollment : classSectionId
Student "1" o-- "*" StudentDocument : studentId
Enrollment "1" o-- "*" StudentDocument : enrollmentId
Student "1" o-- "*" StudentHealthRecord : studentId
Student "1" o-- "*" StudentTransportPreference : studentId
Transportation "1" o-- "*" StudentTransportPreference : preferredBusRouteId
Student "1" o-- "*" StudentInventory : studentId
Curriculum "1" o-- "*" Course : curriculumId

class Asset {
  + {static} assetId : BIGINT <<PK>>
  + schoolId : BIGINT <<FK>>

  + assetNameEn : VARCHAR(200)

  + modelNumber : INT
  + manufacturer : VARCHAR(200)
  + brand : VARCHAR(200)
  + assetCategoryId : BIGINT <<FK>>
  + assetStatusId : BIGINT <<FK>>
  + assetLocationId : BIGINT <<FK>>
  + condition : VARCHAR(200)
  + acquisitionType : VARCHAR(50)
  + acquisitionDate : DATE
  + acquisitionCost : DECIMAL(15,2)
  + currency : VARCHAR(200)
  + supplierName : VARCHAR(200)
  + purchaseOrderReference : VARCHAR(50) <<FK>>
  + warrantyContractId : BIGINT <<FK>>
  + isInsured : BOOLEAN
  + insurancePolicyNumber : INT
  + insuranceExpiryDate : DATE
  + usefulLifeYears : VARCHAR(200)
  + salvageValue : VARCHAR(200)
  + currentValue : VARCHAR(200)
  + barcode : VARCHAR(50)
  + qrCode : VARCHAR(500)
  + rfidTag : VARCHAR(50)
  + hasPhysicalTag : BOOLEAN
  + physicalTagDate : DATE
  + notes : TEXT
  + isActive : BOOLEAN
  + isDeleted : BOOLEAN
  + createdByUserId : BIGINT <<FK>>
  + updatedByUserId : BIGINT <<FK>>
  + createdAt : DATE
  + updatedAt : DATE
  --
  + create()
  + update()
  + delete()
  + findById(id)
  + changeStatus(newStatus)
  + activate()
  + deactivate()
  + assign(department)
}

class EmergencyHosting {
  + {static} hostingId : BIGINT <<PK>>
  + schoolId : BIGINT <<FK>>
  + hostingNumber : INT
  + hostingType : VARCHAR(50)
  + hostingDate : DATE
  + endDate : DATE
  + expectedEndDate : DATE

RemediationPlan "1" o-- "*" EmergencyClosures : compensationPlanId
RemediationPlan "1" o-- "*" SchoolDeficit : relatedRemediationPlanId
RemediationPlan "1" o-- "*" SchoolSurplus : relatedRemediationPlanId
SchoolDeficit "1" o-- "*" RemediationPlan : relatedDeficitId
SchoolSurplus "1" o-- "*" RemediationPlan : relatedSurplusId
@enduml
